Execute the approved plan in the repository.

        Project directory:
        /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/banodoco-workspace/brain-of-bndc

        Idea:
Admin-triggered payments: let the admin mention the bot in a channel to create a payment for a Banodoco user, collect/store the user's Solana wallet privately if needed, send a test payment, use the user's confirmation message to authorize the final payout, and keep all confirmation and money-moving checks fail-closed.

User notes and answers:
- User clarified v1: do NOT require wallet_address in the admin tool call. If no verified Solana wallet is on file for the Banodoco user, create the payment request intent, ping the intended recipient in the originating channel/thread, ask them to reply with their wallet there, and continue only from that recipient's reply. DM path is explicitly deferred for now. Final-payment confirmation should also rely on the intended recipient's message/confirmation in-channel, not DM.
- Revision lock: multiple recipients may have concurrent payment intents in the same channel or thread. Intent matching must be deterministic and identity-gated first by guild_id + source_channel/thread + recipient_user_id + active status before any agent classification runs. The agent should only classify semantics (wallet provided / positive confirmation / ambiguous / negative / strange behavior) after a single candidate intent is identified. Any ambiguity, conflicting signals, suspicious behavior, or out-of-the-ordinary message pattern must fail closed and explicitly notify/tag the admin for review rather than advancing payout.
- Hard revision required before execution: replace in-memory pending intents with a persisted admin_payment_intents table; replace amount_usd-first admin flow with amount_sol/requested_amount_sol as the canonical admin-entered amount; add restart-safe free-text wallet/confirmation recovery using persisted message cursor fields (for example last_scanned_message_id and resolved_by_message_id) plus reconciliation on startup/interval; keep deterministic identity/context gating first and allow multiple concurrent intents in the same thread/channel; the agent may classify semantics only after a single candidate intent is selected; any ambiguity or strange behavior must fail closed and tag the admin; button confirmation may remain as a secondary path but the plan must include free-text confirmation with recovery because the user explicitly asked for it.

        Batch framing:
        - Execute batch 1 of 8.

  "meta_commentary": "This is a large feature spanning 7+ files with a persisted state machine, async reply interception, LLM classification, and startup recovery. Key execution guidance:\n\n**Biggest risk: the Anthropic client bootstrap.** The interceptor fires before _ensure_agent(), so self.agent may be None. The executor MUST create a standalone AsyncAnthropic client in AdminChatCog.__init__ for classification. Do NOT use self.agent.client or call _ensure_agent() from the interceptor \u2014 that would trigger full agent initialization on every non-bot message in a channel with an active intent.\n\n**Button-intent sync is already solved by the existing architecture.** The gate disputed FLAG-ADMIN-PAY-004 but the resolution is correct: _handoff_terminal_result (payment_cog.py:269-299) fires for ALL terminal states, regardless of whether the button or free-text confirmed the payment. handle_payment_result will be called either way and can update the intent row. No PaymentConfirmView changes needed.\n\n**Producer name 'admin_chat' auto-discovers AdminChatCog.** Verified: _candidate_producer_cog_names('admin_chat') generates ['AdminChatCog', ...]. Zero changes to PaymentCog needed for producer routing.\n\n**Duplicate terminal messages concern.** PaymentCog posts a generic notify before _handoff_terminal_result. The producer-specific message from handle_payment_result should go to the same channel but add admin-payment-specific context (intent status, recipient ping). This is the same pattern GrantsCog uses \u2014 the grant-specific message goes to the grant thread while PaymentCog's generic goes to the notify channel. If resolve_payment_destinations returns the same channel for both, the executor should ensure the producer-specific message adds enough value to justify the duplication, or skip it when destinations overlap.\n\n**T4-T5-T10 can run in parallel** with T1-T2-T3 since they touch different files. The executor should do Phase 1 (T1-T3) and the independent tool/prompt work (T4-T5-T10) concurrently, then T6 after both complete.\n\n**Classification prompt design.** Keep it very short: system prompt defining the 5 categories with one-line descriptions, user message is the raw reply text plus brief context (what we asked for). Return JSON `{\"category\": \"...\", \"extracted_address\": \"...\"}`. Use the same model as admin chat. The classification is a single API call, not a chat turn.",

Execute the approved plan in the repository.

        Project directory:
        /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/banodoco-workspace/brain-of-bndc

        Idea:
Admin-triggered payments: let the admin mention the bot in a channel to create a payment for a Banodoco user, collect/store the user's Solana wallet privately if needed, send a test payment, use the user's confirmation message to authorize the final payout, and keep all confirmation and money-moving checks fail-closed.

User notes and answers:
- User clarified v1: do NOT require wallet_address in the admin tool call. If no verified Solana wallet is on file for the Banodoco user, create the payment request intent, ping the intended recipient in the originating channel/thread, ask them to reply with their wallet there, and continue only from that recipient's reply. DM path is explicitly deferred for now. Final-payment confirmation should also rely on the intended recipient's message/confirmation in-channel, not DM.
- Revision lock: multiple recipients may have concurrent payment intents in the same channel or thread. Intent matching must be deterministic and identity-gated first by guild_id + source_channel/thread + recipient_user_id + active status before any agent classification runs. The agent should only classify semantics (wallet provided / positive confirmation / ambiguous / negative / strange behavior) after a single candidate intent is identified. Any ambiguity, conflicting signals, suspicious behavior, or out-of-the-ordinary message pattern must fail closed and explicitly notify/tag the admin for review rather than advancing payout.
- Hard revision required before execution: replace in-memory pending intents with a persisted admin_payment_intents table; replace amount_usd-first admin flow with amount_sol/requested_amount_sol as the canonical admin-entered amount; add restart-safe free-text wallet/confirmation recovery using persisted message cursor fields (for example last_scanned_message_id and resolved_by_message_id) plus reconciliation on startup/interval; keep deterministic identity/context gating first and allow multiple concurrent intents in the same thread/channel; the agent may classify semantics only after a single candidate intent is selected; any ambiguity or strange behavior must fail closed and tag the admin; button confirmation may remain as a secondary path but the plan must include free-text confirmation with recovery because the user explicitly asked for it.

  "meta_commentary": "This is a large feature spanning 7+ files with a persisted state machine, async reply interception, LLM classification, and startup recovery. Key execution guidance:\n\n**Biggest risk: the Anthropic client bootstrap.** The interceptor fires before _ensure_agent(), so self.agent may be None. The executor MUST create a standalone AsyncAnthropic client in AdminChatCog.__init__ for classification. Do NOT use self.agent.client or call _ensure_agent() from the interceptor \u2014 that would trigger full agent initialization on every non-bot message in a channel with an active intent.\n\n**Button-intent sync is already solved by the existing architecture.** The gate disputed FLAG-ADMIN-PAY-004 but the resolution is correct: _handoff_terminal_result (payment_cog.py:269-299) fires for ALL terminal states, regardless of whether the button or free-text confirmed the payment. handle_payment_result will be called either way and can update the intent row. No PaymentConfirmView changes needed.\n\n**Producer name 'admin_chat' auto-discovers AdminChatCog.** Verified: _candidate_producer_cog_names('admin_chat') generates ['AdminChatCog', ...]. Zero changes to PaymentCog needed for producer routing.\n\n**Duplicate terminal messages concern.** PaymentCog posts a generic notify before _handoff_terminal_result. The producer-specific message from handle_payment_result should go to the same channel but add admin-payment-specific context (intent status, recipient ping). This is the same pattern GrantsCog uses \u2014 the grant-specific message goes to the grant thread while PaymentCog's generic goes to the notify channel. If resolve_payment_destinations returns the same channel for both, the executor should ensure the producer-specific message adds enough value to justify the duplication, or skip it when destinations overlap.\n\n**T4-T5-T10 can run in parallel** with T1-T2-T3 since they touch different files.         Project directory:

        Idea:
Admin-triggered payments: let the admin mention the bot in a channel to create a payment for a Banodoco user, collect/store the user's Solana wallet privately if needed, send a test payment, use the user's confirmation message to authorize the final payout, and keep all confirmation and money-moving checks fail-closed.

User notes and answers:
- User clarified v1: do NOT require wallet_address in the admin tool call. If no verified Solana wallet is on file for the Banodoco user, create the payment request intent, ping the intended recipient in the originating channel/thread, ask them to reply with their wallet there, and continue only from that recipient's reply. DM path is explicitly deferred for now. Final-payment confirmation should also rely on the intended recipient's message/confirmation in-channel, not DM.
- Revision lock: multiple recipients may have concurrent payment intents in the same channel or thread. Intent matching must be deterministic and identity-gated first by guild_id + source_channel/thread + recipient_user_id + active status before any agent classification runs. The agent should only classify semantics (wallet provided / positive confirmation / ambiguous / negative / strange behavior) after a single candidate intent is identified. Any ambiguity, conflicting signals, suspicious behavior, or out-of-the-ordinary message pattern must fail closed and explicitly notify/tag the admin for review rather than advancing payout.
- Hard revision required before execution: replace in-memory pending intents with a persisted admin_payment_intents table; replace amount_usd-first admin flow with amount_sol/requested_amount_sol as the canonical admin-entered amount; add restart-safe free-text wallet/confirmation recovery using persisted message cursor fields (for example last_scanned_message_id and resolved_by_message_id) plus reconciliation on startup/interval; keep deterministic identity/context gating first and allow multiple concurrent intents in the same thread/channel; the agent may classify semantics only after a single candidate intent is selected; any ambiguity or strange behavior must fail closed and tag the admin; button confirmation may remain as a secondary path but the plan must include free-text confirmation with recovery because the user explicitly asked for it.

    "FLAG-ADMIN-PAY-004: Button-intent sync \u2014 handle_payment_result MUST update intent to confirmed/completed on final-payment terminal states. This is what closes the gap: _handoff_terminal_result fires for ALL terminal states regardless of whether button or free-text was used. Verify this works by checking payment_cog.py:251-299.",
    "FLAG-ADMIN-PAY-005: Anthropic client bootstrap \u2014 the interceptor (_check_pending_payment_reply) fires BEFORE _ensure_agent(). Create a standalone AsyncAnthropic client in AdminChatCog.__init__ for classification, independent of AdminChatAgent. Do NOT call _ensure_agent() from the interceptor.",
    "DEBT admin-payments-intent-state: The button path does not directly update admin_payment_intents \u2014 it goes PaymentConfirmView \u2192 confirm_payment \u2192 PaymentCog processes to terminal \u2192 _handoff_terminal_result \u2192 handle_payment_result \u2192 updates intent. This indirect path is correct but must be verified end-to-end.",
    "Duplicate terminal messages: PaymentCog._handle_terminal_payment() posts a generic notify message BEFORE calling _handoff_terminal_result. AdminChatCog.handle_payment_result may post its own message. Ensure these go to different channels/threads or that the producer-specific message adds value beyond the generic one.",
    "The unique index on payment_requests (producer, producer_ref, is_test) WHERE status NOT IN ('failed','cancelled') means producer_ref must be unique per active payment. The timestamp-based ref strategy must not collide if the admin initiates two payments within the same second \u2014 consider using a UUID suffix if needed.",
    "Race condition: _processing_intents set prevents double-processing of the same intent reply. Ensure it's used with try/finally to always clean up.",
    "Startup recovery must be rate-limited: max 200 messages per intent per reconciliation pass. Don't scan channels with very old unresolved intents indefinitely.",
    "The classifier prompt must be concise and return structured output (JSON with category field). Use the same model as admin chat but with a very short system prompt focused only on classification."
  ],

  "meta_commentary": "This is a large feature spanning 7+ files with a persisted state machine, async reply interception, LLM classification, and startup recovery. Key execution guidance:\n\n**Biggest risk: the Anthropic client bootstrap.** The interceptor fires before _ensure_agent(), so self.agent may be None. The executor MUST create a standalone AsyncAnthropic client in AdminChatCog.__init__ for classification. Do NOT use self.agent.client or call _ensure_agent() from the interceptor \u2014 that would trigger full agent initialization on every non-bot message in a channel with an active intent.\n\n**Button-intent sync is already solved by the existing architecture.** The gate disputed FLAG-ADMIN-PAY-004 but the resolution is correct: _handoff_terminal_result (payment_cog.py:269-299) fires for ALL terminal states, regardless of whether the button or free-text confirmed the payment. handle_payment_result will be called either way and can update the intent row. No PaymentConfirmView changes needed.\n\n**Producer name 'admin_chat' auto-discovers AdminChatCog.** Verified: _candidate_producer_cog_names('admin_chat') generates ['AdminChatCog', ...]. Zero changes to PaymentCog needed for producer routing.\n\n**Duplicate terminal messages concern.** PaymentCog posts a generic notify before _handoff_terminal_result. The producer-specific message from handle_payment_result should go to the same channel but add admin-payment-specific context (intent status, recipient ping). This is the same pattern GrantsCog uses \u2014 the grant-specific message goes to the grant thread while PaymentCog's generic goes to the notify channel. If resolve_payment_destinations returns the same channel for both, the executor should ensure the producer-specific message adds enough value to justify the duplication, or skip it when destinations overlap.\n\n**T4-T5-T10 can run in parallel** with T1-T2-T3 since they touch different files. The executor should do Phase 1 (T1-T3) and the independent tool/prompt work (T4-T5-T10) concurrently, then T6 after both complete.\n\n**Classification prompt design.** Keep it very short: system prompt defining the 5 categories with one-line descriptions, user message is the raw reply text plus brief context (what we asked for). Return JSON `{\"category\": \"...\", \"extracted_address\": \"...\"}`. Use the same model as admin chat. The classification is a single API call, not a chat turn.",
  "validation": {

        Project directory:
        /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/banodoco-workspace/brain-of-bndc

        Idea:
Admin-triggered payments: let the admin mention the bot in a channel to create a payment for a Banodoco user, collect/store the user's Solana wallet privately if needed, send a test payment, use the user's confirmation message to authorize the final payout, and keep all confirmation and money-moving checks fail-closed.

User notes and answers:
- User clarified v1: do NOT require wallet_address in the admin tool call. If no verified Solana wallet is on file for the Banodoco user, create the payment request intent, ping the intended recipient in the originating channel/thread, ask them to reply with their wallet there, and continue only from that recipient's reply. DM path is explicitly deferred for now. Final-payment confirmation should also rely on the intended recipient's message/confirmation in-channel, not DM.
- Revision lock: multiple recipients may have concurrent payment intents in the same channel or thread. Intent matching must be deterministic and identity-gated first by guild_id + source_channel/thread + recipient_user_id + active status before any agent classification runs. The agent should only classify semantics (wallet provided / positive confirmation / ambiguous / negative / strange behavior) after a single candidate intent is identified. Any ambiguity, conflicting signals, suspicious behavior, or out-of-the-ordinary message pattern must fail closed and explicitly notify/tag the admin for review rather than advancing payout.
- Hard revision required before execution: replace in-memory pending intents with a persisted admin_payment_intents table; replace amount_usd-first admin flow with amount_sol/requested_amount_sol as the canonical admin-entered amount; add restart-safe free-text wallet/confirmation recovery using persisted message cursor fields (for example last_scanned_message_id and resolved_by_message_id) plus reconciliation on startup/interval; keep deterministic identity/context gating first and allow multiple concurrent intents in the same thread/channel; the agent may classify semantics only after a single candidate intent is selected; any ambiguity or strange behavior must fail closed and tag the admin; button confirmation may remain as a secondary path but the plan must include free-text confirmation with recovery because the user explicitly asked for it.

'ambiguous: intent is unclear, mixed, or missing a usable wallet',\n+                'suspicious: strange, manipulative, off-topic, or abnormal behavior',\n+            ],\n+            'awaiting_confirmation': [\n+                'positive_confirmation: the user clearly approves the payout',\n+                'declined: the user refuses or asks to cancel',\n+                'ambiguous: intent is unclear, mixed, or conditional',\n+                'suspicious: strange, manipulative, off-topic, or abnormal behavior',\n+            ],\n+        }\n+        system = (\n+            \"Classify one Discord reply for an admin payment flow. \"\n+            \"Return JSON only with keys category and extracted_address. \"\n+            f\"Allowed categories: {'; getattr(block, 'type', None) == 'text'\n+            ).strip()\n+            start = raw.find('{')\n+            end = raw.rfind('}')\n+            payload = json.loads(raw[start:end + 1] if start !=         Idea:
Admin-triggered payments: let the admin mention the bot in a channel to create a payment for a Banodoco user, collect/store the user's Solana wallet privately if needed, send a test payment, use the user's confirmation message to authorize the final payout, and keep all confirmation and money-moving checks fail-closed.

User notes and answers:
- User clarified v1: do NOT require wallet_address in the admin tool call. If no verified Solana wallet is on file for the Banodoco user, create the payment request intent, ping the intended recipient in the originating channel/thread, ask them to reply with their wallet there, and continue only from that recipient's reply. DM path is explicitly deferred for now. Final-payment confirmation should also rely on the intended recipient's message/confirmation in-channel, not DM.
- Revision lock: multiple recipients may have concurrent payment intents in the same channel or thread. Intent matching must be deterministic and identity-gated first by guild_id + source_channel/thread + recipient_user_id + active status before any agent classification runs. The agent should only classify semantics (wallet provided / positive confirmation / ambiguous / negative / strange behavior) after a single candidate intent is identified. Any ambiguity, conflicting signals, suspicious behavior, or out-of-the-ordinary message pattern must fail closed and explicitly notify/tag the admin for review rather than advancing payout.
- Hard revision required before execution: replace in-memory pending intents with a persisted admin_payment_intents table; replace amount_usd-first admin flow with amount_sol/requested_amount_sol as the canonical admin-entered amount; add restart-safe free-text wallet/confirmation recovery using persisted message cursor fields (for example last_scanned_message_id and resolved_by_message_id) plus reconciliation on startup/interval; keep deterministic identity/context gating first and allow multiple concurrent intents in the same thread/channel; the agent may classify semantics only after a single candidate intent is selected; any ambiguity or strange behavior must fail closed and tag the admin; button confirmation may remain as a secondary path but the plan must include free-text confirmation with recovery because the user explicitly asked for it.

intent, \"The payout completed, but I could not mark the intent as completed.\", fail_intent=False)\n+            return\n+\n+        if channel:\n+            await self._notify_admin_review(\n+                channel,\n+                intent,\n+                f\"The final payout ended in `{status}`, so I stopped the flow for manual review.\",\n+            )\n+        else:\n+            self.db_handler.update_admin_payment_intent(intent_id, {'status': 'failed'}, guild_id)\n \n     async def _check_pending_payment_reply(self, message: discord.Message) -> bool:\n         \"\"\"Intercept recipient replies for active admin payment intents before normal bot routing.\"\"\"\n         if message.author.bot or message.guild is None:\n             return False\n*** End Patch\n"
}

> DEVELOPER

Execute the approved plan in the repository.

        Project directory:
        /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/banodoco-workspace/brain-of-bndc

        Idea:
Admin-triggered payments: let the admin mention the bot in a channel to create a payment for a Banodoco user, collect/store the user's Solana wallet privately if needed, send a test payment, use the user's confirmation message to authorize the final payout, and keep all confirmation and money-moving checks fail-closed.

User notes and answers:
- User clarified v1: do NOT require wallet_address in the admin tool call. If no verified Solana wallet is on file for the Banodoco user, create the payment request intent, ping the intended recipient in the originating channel/thread, ask them to reply with their wallet there, and continue only from that recipient's reply. DM path is explicitly deferred for now. Final-payment confirmation should also rely on the intended recipient's message/confirmation in-channel, not DM.
- Revision lock: multiple recipients may have concurrent payment intents in the same channel or thread. Intent matching must be deterministic and identity-gated first by guild_id + source_channel/thread + recipient_user_id + active status before any agent classification runs. The agent should only classify semantics (wallet provided / positive confirmation / ambiguous / negative / strange behavior) after a single candidate intent is identified. Any ambiguity, conflicting signals, suspicious behavior, or out-of-the-ordinary message pattern must fail closed and explicitly notify/tag the admin for review rather than advancing payout.
- Hard revision required before execution: replace in-memory pending intents with a persisted admin_payment_intents table; replace amount_usd-first admin flow with amount_sol/requested_amount_sol as the canonical admin-entered amount; add restart-safe free-text wallet/confirmation recovery using persisted message cursor fields (for example last_scanned_message_id and resolved_by_message_id) plus reconciliation on startup/interval; keep deterministic identity/context gating first and allow multiple concurrent intents in the same thread/channel; the agent may classify semantics only after a single candidate intent is selected; any ambiguity or strange behavior must fail closed and tag the admin; button confirmation may remain as a secondary path but the plan must include free-text confirmation with recovery because the user explicitly asked for it.

  "watch_items": [
    "FLAG-ADMIN-PAY-004: Button-intent sync \u2014 handle_payment_result MUST update intent to confirmed/completed on final-payment terminal states. This is what closes the gap: _handoff_terminal_result fires for ALL terminal states regardless of whether button or free-text was used. Verify this works by checking payment_cog.py:251-299.",
    "FLAG-ADMIN-PAY-005: Anthropic client bootstrap \u2014 the interceptor (_check_pending_payment_reply) fires BEFORE _ensure_agent(). Create a standalone AsyncAnthropic client in AdminChatCog.__init__ for classification, independent of AdminChatAgent. Do NOT call _ensure_agent() from the interceptor.",
    "DEBT admin-payments-intent-state: The button path does not directly update admin_payment_intents \u2014 it goes PaymentConfirmView \u2192 confirm_payment \u2192 PaymentCog processes to terminal \u2192 _handoff_terminal_result \u2192 handle_payment_result \u2192 updates intent. This indirect path is correct but must be verified end-to-end.",
    "Duplicate terminal messages: PaymentCog._handle_terminal_payment() posts a generic notify message BEFORE calling _handoff_terminal_result. AdminChatCog.handle_payment_result may post its own message. Ensure these go to different channels/threads or that the producer-specific message adds value beyond the generic one.",
    "The unique index on payment_requests (producer, producer_ref, is_test) WHERE status NOT IN ('failed','cancelled') means producer_ref must be unique per active payment. The timestamp-based ref strategy must not collide if the admin initiates two payments within the same second \u2014 consider using a UUID suffix if needed.",
    "Race condition: _processing_intents set prevents double-processing of the same intent reply. Ensure it's used with try/finally to always clean up.",
    "Startup recovery must be rate-limited: max 200 messages per intent per reconciliation pass. Don't scan channels with very old unresolved intents indefinitely.",
    "The classifier prompt must be concise and return structured output (JSON with category field). Use the same model as admin chat but with a very short system prompt focused only on classification."
  ],

  "meta_commentary": "This is a large feature spanning 7+ files with a persisted state machine, async reply interception, LLM classification, and startup recovery. Key execution guidance:\n\n**Biggest risk: the Anthropic client bootstrap.** The interceptor fires before _ensure_agent(), so self.agent may be None. The executor MUST create a standalone AsyncAnthropic client in AdminChatCog.__init__ for classification. Do NOT use self.agent.client or call _ensure_agent() from the interceptor \u2014 that would trigger full agent initialization on every non-bot message in a channel with an active intent.\n\n**Button-intent sync is already solved by the existing architecture.** The gate disputed FLAG-ADMIN-PAY-004 but the resolution is correct: _handoff_terminal_result (payment_cog.py:269-299) fires for ALL terminal states, regardless of whether the button or free-text confirmed the payment. handle_payment_result will be called either way and can update the intent row. No PaymentConfirmView changes needed.\n\n**Producer name 'admin_chat' auto-discovers AdminChatCog.** Verified: _candidate_producer_cog_names('admin_chat') generates ['AdminChatCog', ...]. Zero changes to PaymentCog needed for producer routing.\n\n**Duplicate terminal messages concern.** PaymentCog posts a generic notify before _handoff_terminal_result. The producer-specific message from handle_payment_result should go to the same channel but add admin-payment-specific context (intent status, recipient ping). This is the same pattern GrantsCog uses \u2014 the grant-specific message goes to the grant thread while PaymentCog's generic goes to the notify channel. If resolve_payment_destinations returns the same channel for both, the executor should ensure the producer-specific message adds enough value to justify the duplication, or skip it when destinations overlap.\n\n**T4-T5-T10 can run in parallel** with T1-T2-T3 since they touch different files. The executor should do Phase 1 (T1-T3) and the independent tool/prompt work (T4-T5-T10) concurrently, then T6 after both complete.\n\n**Classification prompt design.** Keep it very short: system prompt defining the 5 categories with one-line descriptions, user message is the raw reply text plus brief context (what we asked for). Return JSON `{\"category\": \"...\", \"extracted_address\": \"...\"}`. Use the same model as admin chat. The classification is a single API call, not a chat turn.",

'write_enabled': True}]\n\nclass FakeAuthor:\n    def __init__(self, user_id):\n        self.id = user_id\n        self.bot = False\n\nclass FakeMessage:\n    def __init__(self, message_id, author_id, channel, guild):\n        self.id = message_id\n        self.author = FakeAuthor(author_id)\n        self.channel = channel\n        self.guild = guild\n        self.content = 'message'\n\nclass FakeChannel:\n    def __init__(self, channel_id, guild, messages):\n        self.id = channel_id\n        self.guild = guild\n        self._messages = list(messages)\n    def history(self, limit=100, after=None, oldest_first=False):\n        after_id = getattr(after, 'id', None)\n        items = [m for m in self._messages if after_id is None or m.id > after_id]\n        items.sort(key=lambda m: m.id, reverse=not oldest_first)\n        items = items[:limit]\n        async def iterator():\n            for item in items:\n                yield item\n        return iterator()\n\nclass FakeDB:\n    def __init__(self):\n        self.server_config = FakeServerConfig()\n        self.intents = {\n            'intent-history': {\n                'intent_id': 'intent-history',\n                'guild_id': 1,\n                'channel_id': 77,\n                'recipient_user_id': 42,\n                'status': 'awaiting_confirmation',\n                'prompt_message_id': 1000,\n                'last_scanned_message_id': None,\n                'final_payment_id': 'pay-pending',\n            },\n            'intent-missing': {\n                'intent_id': 'intent-missing',\n                'guild_id': 1,\n                'channel_id': 99,\n                'recipient_user_id': 88,\n                'status': 'awaiting_wallet',\n                'prompt_message_id': 2000,\n                'last_scanned_message_id': None,\n            },\n            'intent-test': {\n                'intent_id': 'intent-test',\n                'guild_id': 1,\n                'channel_id': 77,\n                'recipient_user_id': 42,\n                'status': 'awaiting_test',\n                'test_payment_id': 'pay-test',\n            },\n            'intent-final': {\n                'intent_id': 'intent-final',\n                'guild_id': 1,\n                'channel_id': 77,\n                'recipient_user_id': 42,\n                'status': 'confirmed',\n                'final_payment_id': 'pay-final',\n            },\n        }\n        self.updates = []\n    def list_active_intents(self, guild_id):\n        return [dict(intent) for intent in self.intents.values() if intent['guild_id'] == guild_id]\n    def get_admin_payment_intent(self, intent_id, guild_id):\n        intent = self.intents.get(intent_id)\n        if not intent or intent['guild_id'] != guild_id:\n            return None\n        return dict(intent)\n    def update_admin_payment_intent(self, intent_id, payload, guild_id):\n        intent = self.intents[intent_id]\n        assert intent['guild_id'] == guild_id\n        intent.update(payload)\n        self.updates.append((intent_id, dict(payload), guild_id))\n        return dict(intent)\n    def get_payment_request(self, payment_id, guild_id=None):\n        payments = {\n            'pay-pending': {'payment_id': 'pay-pending', 'producer': 'admin_chat', 'guild_id': 1, 'status': 'queued'},\n            'pay-test': {'payment_id': 'pay-test', 'producer': 'admin_chat', 'guild_id': 1, 'status': 'confirmed', 'is_test': True, 'metadata': {'intent_id': 'intent-test'}},\n            'pay-final': {'payment_id': 'pay-final', 'producer': 'admin_chat', 'guild_id': 1, 'status': 'confirmed', 'is_test': False, 'metadata': {'intent_id': 'intent-final'}},\n        }\n        return payments.get(payment_id)\n\nclass FakeBot:\n    def __init__(self, channel):\n        self._channel = channel\n        self.wait_calls = 0\n        self.guilds = [channel.guild]\n        self.payment_service = object()\n    async def wait_until_ready(self):\n        self.wait_calls += 1\n    def get_channel(self, channel_id):\n        return self._channel if channel_id == self._channel.id else None\n    async def fetch_channel(self, channel_id):\n        return self._channel if channel_id == self._channel.id else None\n\nasync def main():\n    guild = SimpleNamespace(id=1)\n    channel = FakeChannel(77, guild, [])\n    messages = [FakeMessage(message_id, 42, channel, guild) for message_id in range(1001, 1206)]\n    channel._messages = messages\n\n    db = FakeDB()\n    bot = FakeBot(channel)\n    cog = AdminChatCog(bot, db, sharer=object())\n\n    replayed = []\n    terminal_handoffs = []\n\n    async def fake_check(message):\n        replayed.append(message.id)\n        return True\n    async def fake_handle(payment):\n        terminal_handoffs.append(payment['payment_id'])\n\n    cog._check_pending_payment_reply = fake_check\n    cog.handle_payment_result = fake_handle\n\n    await cog.cog_load()\n\n    assert bot.wait_calls == 1\n    assert replayed == list(range(1001, 1201))\n    assert db.intents['intent-history']['last_scanned_message_id'] == 1200\n    assert Execute the approved plan in the repository.

        Project directory:
        /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/banodoco-workspace/brain-of-bndc

        Idea:
Admin-triggered payments: let the admin mention the bot in a channel to create a payment for a Banodoco user, collect/store the user's Solana wallet privately if needed, send a test payment, use the user's confirmation message to authorize the final payout, and keep all confirmation and money-moving checks fail-closed.

User notes and answers:
- User clarified v1: do NOT require wallet_address in the admin tool call. If no verified Solana wallet is on file for the Banodoco user, create the payment request intent, ping the intended recipient in the originating channel/thread, ask them to reply with their wallet there, and continue only from that recipient's reply. DM path is explicitly deferred for now. Final-payment confirmation should also rely on the intended recipient's message/confirmation in-channel, not DM.
- Revision lock: multiple recipients may have concurrent payment intents in the same channel or thread. Intent matching must be deterministic and identity-gated first by guild_id + source_channel/thread + recipient_user_id + active status before any agent classification runs. The agent should only classify semantics (wallet provided / positive confirmation / ambiguous / negative / strange behavior) after a single candidate intent is identified. Any ambiguity, conflicting signals, suspicious behavior, or out-of-the-ordinary message pattern must fail closed and explicitly notify/tag the admin for review rather than advancing payout.
- Hard revision required before execution: replace in-memory pending intents with a persisted admin_payment_intents table; replace amount_usd-first admin flow with amount_sol/requested_amount_sol as the canonical admin-entered amount; add restart-safe free-text wallet/confirmation recovery using persisted message cursor fields (for example last_scanned_message_id and resolved_by_message_id) plus reconciliation on startup/interval; keep deterministic identity/context gating first and allow multiple concurrent intents in the same thread/channel; the agent may classify semantics only after a single candidate intent is selected; any ambiguity or strange behavior must fail closed and tag the admin; button confirmation may remain as a secondary path but the plan must include free-text confirmation with recovery because the user explicitly asked for it.

The executor MUST create a standalone AsyncAnthropic client in AdminChatCog.__init__ for classification. Do NOT use self.agent.client or call _ensure_agent() from the interceptor \u2014 that would trigger full agent initialization on every non-bot message in a channel with an active intent.\n\n**Button-intent sync is already solved by the existing architecture.** The gate disputed FLAG-ADMIN-PAY-004 but the resolution is correct: _handoff_terminal_result (payment_cog.py:269-299) fires for ALL terminal states, regardless of whether the button or free-text confirmed the payment. handle_payment_result will be called either way and can update the intent row. No PaymentConfirmView changes needed.\n\n**Producer name 'admin_chat' auto-discovers AdminChatCog.** Verified: _candidate_producer_cog_names('admin_chat') generates ['AdminChatCog', ...]. Zero changes to PaymentCog needed for producer routing.\n\n**Duplicate terminal messages concern.** PaymentCog posts a generic notify before _handoff_terminal_result. FakeBot(channel, payment_service=object())\n+    cog = AdminChatCog(bot, db, sharer=object())\n+    matched = []\n+\n+    async def record_wallet(message, intent, wallet_address):\n+        matched.append((message.author.id, intent[\"intent_id\"], wallet_address))\n+\n+    cog._handle_wallet_received = record_wallet\n+\n+    await cog._check_pending_payment_reply(FakeMessage(1005, FakeAuthor(42), channel, guild, VALID_SOL_ADDRESS))\n+    await cog._check_pending_payment_reply(FakeMessage(1006, FakeAuthor(43), channel, guild, VALID_SOL_ADDRESS))\n+\n+    assert matched == [\n+        (42, intent_one[\"intent_id\"], VALID_SOL_ADDRESS),\n+        (43, intent_two[\"intent_id\"], VALID_SOL_ADDRESS),\n+    ]\n+\n+\n+@pytest.mark.anyio\n+async def test_request_payment_amount_token():\n+    provider = FakeProvider()\n+    db = FakePaymentRequestDB()\n+    service = PaymentService(db_handler=db, providers={\"solana\": provider}, test_payment_amount=0.01)\n+\n+    result = await service.request_payment(\n+        producer=\"admin_chat\",\n+        producer_ref=\"intent-1\",\n+        guild_id=1,\n+        recipient_wallet=VALID_SOL_ADDRESS,\n+        chain=\"solana\",\n+        provider=\"solana\",\n+        is_test=False,\n+        amount_token=1.5,\n+        confirm_channel_id=55,\n+        notify_channel_id=55,\n+        recipient_discord_id=42,\n+        wallet_id=\"wallet-1\",\n+        metadata={\"intent_id\": \"intent-1\"},\n+    )\n+\n+    assert result is not None\n+    assert result[\"amount_token\"] == 1.5\n+    assert result[\"amount_usd\"] is None\n+    assert result[\"token_price_usd\"] is None\n+    assert provider.price_calls == 0\n+\n+\n+@pytest.mark.anyio\n+async def test_startup_reconciliation():\n+    guild = SimpleNamespace(id=1)\n+    channel = FakeChannel(channel_id=55, guild=guild)\n+    db = FakeIntentDB()\n+    intent = db.create_admin_payment_intent(\n+        {\n+            \"channel_id\": 55,\n+            \"recipient_user_id\": 42,\n+            \"requested_amount_sol\": 1.75,\n+            \"producer_ref\": \"1_42_123\",\n+            \"final_payment_id\": \"payment-final\",\n+            \"prompt_message_id\": 1000,\n+            \"status\": \"awaiting_confirmation\",\n+        },\n+        guild_id=1,\n+    )\n+    payment_service = FakeAdminPaymentService(confirm_result={\"payment_id\": \"payment-final\", \"status\": \"queued\"})\n+    bot = FakeBot(channel, payment_service=payment_service)\n+    cog = AdminChatCog(bot, db, sharer=object())\n+    cog.payment_service = payment_service\n+    cog._classifier_client = FakeClassifierClient('{\"category\":\"positive_confirmation\",\"extracted_address\":null}')\n+    message = FakeMessage(1001, FakeAuthor(42), channel, guild, \"yes\")\n+    channel._messages = [message]\n+\n+    await cog.cog_load()\n+\n+    assert bot.ready_waits == 1\n+    assert db.intents[intent[\"intent_id\"]][\"status\"] == \"confirmed\"\n+    assert db.intents[intent[\"intent_id\"]][\"last_scanned_message_id\"] == 1001\n+    assert payment_service.confirm_calls == [\n+        (\"payment-final\", {\"guild_id\": 1, \"confirmed_by\": \"free_text\", \"confirmed_by_user_id\": 42})\n+    ]\n*** End Patch\n"

Execute the approved plan in the repository.

        Project directory:
        /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/banodoco-workspace/brain-of-bndc

        Idea:
Admin-triggered payments: let the admin mention the bot in a channel to create a payment for a Banodoco user, collect/store the user's Solana wallet privately if needed, send a test payment, use the user's confirmation message to authorize the final payout, and keep all confirmation and money-moving checks fail-closed.

User notes and answers:
- User clarified v1: do NOT require wallet_address in the admin tool call. If no verified Solana wallet is on file for the Banodoco user, create the payment request intent, ping the intended recipient in the originating channel/thread, ask them to reply with their wallet there, and continue only from that recipient's reply. DM path is explicitly deferred for now. Final-payment confirmation should also rely on the intended recipient's message/confirmation in-channel, not DM.
- Revision lock: multiple recipients may have concurrent payment intents in the same channel or thread. Intent matching must be deterministic and identity-gated first by guild_id + source_channel/thread + recipient_user_id + active status before any agent classification runs. The agent should only classify semantics (wallet provided / positive confirmation / ambiguous / negative / strange behavior) after a single candidate intent is identified. Any ambiguity, conflicting signals, suspicious behavior, or out-of-the-ordinary message pattern must fail closed and explicitly notify/tag the admin for review rather than advancing payout.
- Hard revision required before execution: replace in-memory pending intents with a persisted admin_payment_intents table; replace amount_usd-first admin flow with amount_sol/requested_amount_sol as the canonical admin-entered amount; add restart-safe free-text wallet/confirmation recovery using persisted message cursor fields (for example last_scanned_message_id and resolved_by_message_id) plus reconciliation on startup/interval; keep deterministic identity/context gating first and allow multiple concurrent intents in the same thread/channel; the agent may classify semantics only after a single candidate intent is selected; any ambiguity or strange behavior must fail closed and tag the admin; button confirmation may remain as a secondary path but the plan must include free-text confirmation with recovery because the user explicitly asked for it.

  "meta_commentary": "This is a large feature spanning 7+ files with a persisted state machine, async reply interception, LLM classification, and startup recovery. Key execution guidance:\n\n**Biggest risk: the Anthropic client bootstrap.** The interceptor fires before _ensure_agent(), so self.agent may be None. The executor MUST create a standalone AsyncAnthropic client in AdminChatCog.__init__ for classification. Do NOT use self.agent.client or call _ensure_agent() from the interceptor \u2014 that would trigger full agent initialization on every non-bot message in a channel with an active intent.\n\n**Button-intent sync is already solved by the existing architecture.** The gate disputed FLAG-ADMIN-PAY-004 but the resolution is correct: _handoff_terminal_result (payment_cog.py:269-299) fires for ALL terminal states, regardless of whether the button or free-text confirmed the payment. handle_payment_result will be called either way and can update the intent row. No PaymentConfirmView changes needed.\n\n**Producer name 'admin_chat' auto-discovers AdminChatCog.** Verified: _candidate_producer_cog_names('admin_chat') generates ['AdminChatCog', ...]. Zero changes to PaymentCog needed for producer routing.\n\n**Duplicate terminal messages concern.** PaymentCog posts a generic notify before _handoff_terminal_result. The producer-specific message from handle_payment_result should go to the same channel but add admin-payment-specific context (intent status, recipient ping). This is the same pattern GrantsCog uses \u2014 the grant-specific message goes to the grant thread while PaymentCog's generic goes to the notify channel. If resolve_payment_destinations returns the same channel for both, the executor should ensure the producer-specific message adds enough value to justify the duplication, or skip it when destinations overlap.\n\n**T4-T5-T10 can run in parallel** with T1-T2-T3 since they touch different files. The executor should do Phase 1 (T1-T3) and the independent tool/prompt work (T4-T5-T10) concurrently, then T6 after both complete.\n\n**Classification prompt design.** Keep it very short: system prompt defining the 5 categories with one-line descriptions, user message is the raw reply text plus brief context (what we asked for). Return JSON `{\"category\": \"...\", \"extracted_address\": \"...\"}`. Use the same model as admin chat. The classification is a single API call, not a chat turn.",
